Function `fft_conv(a)`:
 - n=len(a); size=1<<((2*n-1)-1).bit_length(); F=rfft(a, size); return irfft(F*F,size)[:2*n-1].

 - ensure nonneg sum maybe normalize? For internal a normalized sum1. b=fft_conv(a); return 2*n*b.max().

Projected gradient exact:
```
def proj_grad_opt(a0, time_limit, top_k_seq=[5,10,30,100], ...):
    a = normalize(a0)
    best = a, score
    lr=0.2
    no_improve=0
    while time<...
       b=conv(a); M=b.max(); score=2nM; update
       # weights
       for topk in topk_seq maybe cycle
          w = topk_weights(b, topk) # softmax top but zeros?
          grad=2*cross(w,a)
          # projected direction
          v = a - lr*grad
          d = simplex_project(v) - a
          # line search t in [1,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625]
          for t:
             a_t = a + t*d
             if norm < min: continue
             score_t=2n*conv(a_t).max()
             choose best_t
          if score_t < best - 1e-12: accept, no_improve=0
          else: no_improve++; lr *=0.7; if no_improve>20: lr=0.3? topk changes; if lr<1e-5 break
```
Project simplex function O(n log n) sorting; per iter O(n log n). Since n=1024, fine.

Let's think about `simplex_project` algorithm. We need normalize to sum1. Standard:
```
def simplex_project(v):
    n=len(v); mu=0; rsorted=np.sort(v)[::-1]; cssv=np.cumsum(rsorted); cond = 1+np.arange(1,n+1)*rsorted > cssv; if any: rho=argmax? Use:
for idx in range(n-1,-1,-1)?
```
Robust vectorized:
```
u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
cssv=np.cumsum(u)
cond = u * np.arange(1,n+1) > (cssv - 1)
if cond.any():
    rho = np.where(cond)[0][-1]
    theta=(cssv[rho]-1)/(rho+1)
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
else:
    return np.ones(n)/n
```
This projects onto sum=1. Test if v all negative: cond false, returns uniform; projection of all negative onto simplex is uniform? Actually if v=[-1,-1], projection is [0.5,0.5], yes. Good.

If v already sum1 and nonneg, returns same (theta maybe). We can multiply back? Since we maintain sum1.

For f with Fourier series f=Σ c_m e^{2π i m x}, c_{-m}=conj, c_0=1. The linear convolution h(x) for x∈[0,1] is not simply periodic; but can be expressed using periodic autocorrelation minus tail? For f on [0,1] and linear conv h_+(x)=∫_0^x f(t)f(x-t)dt. For periodic extension F, the circular convolution C(x)=∫_0^1 f(t)f(x-t mod1)dt = Σ c_m c_{-m} e^{2π i m x}? Specifically C(x)=Σ_m |c_m|^2 e^{2π i m x} (c_0=1). The linear h_+(x) for x∈[0,1] differs from C(x) by subtracting integral over t such that x-t outside [0,1], i.e. negative? For x∈[0,1], circular conv includes t∈[0,1] with f(x-t mod1)=f(x-t) for t≤x and f(1+x-t) for t>x. So C(x)=h_+(x)+∫_x^1 f(t) f(x+1-t) dt. That tail is cross between ends. So C=h+tail. If we can make C flat (circle ideal), and tail small? For nonnegative, tail nonnegative. To minimize h max, maybe make C flat low and tail small. For f=mean1, C_0 includes |c|^2. Average C=1. - Phase C: For top best, try length scaling: if best length < 3072, interpolate to 2048/3072/4096 and run L-BFGS + proj grad. Keep best.
- Phase D: Final random restarts with short projected gradient if time.
- Final evaluate and return list floats.

Random Fourier log generation:
```
def random_fourier_init(n, rng, Kmax=8, amp_max=1.0, use_parent=None):
    theta = np.zeros(n)
    K = rng.integers(3, Kmax+1)
    for m in range(1,K+1):
        amp = amp_max * rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0) * (1.0 / np.sqrt(m))
        phase = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
        theta += amp * np.cos(2*np.pi*m*np.arange(n)/n + phase)
    # optionally add noise
    theta += rng.standard_normal(n)*0.1
    # center
    theta -= theta.mean()
    return softmax(theta)
```
This produces smooth nonnegative. Need maybe include low-frequency drift/edge effects: allow cos(0)? scale invariant. Higher m up to maybe n//4? But random high freq creates spikiness; L-BFGS can smooth? Maybe K up to 32 for diversity. However, high m may make θ rough; softmax positive but a may oscillate. Could be good. Use K low/mid.

Also generate "parent DCT + random": take theta0=log(parent_interp) - mean; zero FFT above K=32; add random low K coefficients; a=softmax(theta). This preserves global shape but perturbs.

Also generate "phase-shifted harmonic" seeds: for m=1 with amplitude 0.2-0.9, phase random; m=2 small; etc.

We need generate limited number due time. Maybe 8-12 seeds for n=2048, 4-6 for parent len, 4 for other lengths.

L-BFGS run function:
```
def lbfgs_refine(a0, tau_frac_sequence, maxiter_per_run, budget=None):
    a=normalize(a0)
    theta = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
    theta -= np.mean(theta)
    best_a=a; best_score=score(a)
    for fr in tau_frac_sequence:
        tau = 1/(M*fr) maybe recompute M? Use current M.
        _LB_STATE['tau']=tau
        res=minimize(_lbf_obj, theta, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=_lbf_jac, options={'maxiter':..., 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':20, 'disp':False})
        theta = res.x
        a=softmax(theta)
        score update
        # center theta to prevent drift
        theta -= np.mean(theta)
        # optional line search among theta and old? 
    return best_a,best_score
```

Project gradient exact function details:
```
def exact_proj_grad(a0, time_limit, initial_lr=0.2, verbose=False):
    n=len(a0); a=normalize(a0); best_a=a; best_score=score(a)
    lr=0.2
    it=0; no_improve=0
    fr=0.2  # temperature fraction for weights
    while time<deadline:
       b=conv(a); M=b.max(); score=2nM; update
       temp = max(M*fr, 1e-15)
       weights = exp((b-M)/temp); sum; 
       grad=2*cross(weights,a)
       # scale lr by gradient RMS maybe
       gnorm = np.linalg.norm(grad)
       if gnorm < 1e-15: break
       # direction v = a - lr * grad / (gnorm? maybe) 
       # If lr dimensionless times a? grad entries ≤? For uniform, grad maybe? Let's normalize grad to sum? Use step = a - lr*grad / scale where scale = max(gnorm, eps). But line search covers.
       v = a - lr * grad
       v_proj = simplex_project(v)
       d = v_proj - a
       if np.max(np.abs(d)) < 1e-15: break
       # line search t candidates
       best_t=0; best_s=score
       for t in [1.0,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02]:
           a_t = a + t*d
           # due round, might tiny negative; clip and renorm? Since convex combo, nonneg; float maybe -1e-16.
           if np.any(a_t < -1e-12): continue
           a_t = np.maximum(a_t,0); s=a_t.sum(); if s<0.01 continue; a_t=a_t/s
           b_t=conv(a_t); s_t=2n*b_t.max()
           if s_t < best_s - 1e-15:
                best_s=s_t; best_t=t; best_at=a_t
       if best_t>0:
           a=best_at.copy()
           if best_s < best_score-1e-14: best_score=best_s; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
           else: no_improve += 1 # accepted maybe not best due M? Actually if accepted local score not global best? line search picks best along direction; if not global update, perhaps no improve.
           # adjust lr
           if best_t < 0.5: lr = max(1e-6, lr*0.8)
           else: lr = min(1.0, lr*1.05)
       else:
           no_improve +=1
           lr *=0.5
           if no_improve % 20 ==0: fr *= 0.7 or change? If too sharp, weights top one; maybe increase fr. If no improve, increase fr (smoother) sometimes. Maintain fr between 0.05 and 1.
           if lr < 1e-6: break
    return best_a,best_score
```
But line search evaluating many FFTs per iter. For n=2048, 8 evals * maybe 0.01s=0.08s per iter. 1000 iterations=80s. Good. Could do 2000=160s.

We can reduce line search candidates to [1,0.5,0.25,0.1]. Use best. We can also use previous best t.

```
def get_fft_size(n):
    return 1 << (max(1,n)-1).bit_length()
```

2. `fast_convolve(a)`:
```
def fast_convolve(a):
    n = len(a)
    size = 1 << (2*n - 2).bit_length()  # need size >= 2n-1; (2n-2).bit_length yields n bits; for 2n-1? If 2n-1 = 3, (2).bit_length=2 ->1<<2=4>=3; for 2n-1=5, (4).bit_length=3 ->8>=5. Good.
    F = rfft(a, size)
    return irfft(F*F, size)[:2*n-1]
```
For cross conv length 3n-2, size = 1 << (3*n - 3).bit_length()? Need >=3n-2. Use `target = 3*n - 2; size=1 << (target-1).bit_length()` if target>0 else1. For n=1 target1. Good.

```
return a/s
```


6. `soft_score_weights(a, tau)`:
```
b=fast_convolve(a)
bmax=b.max()
if tau <=0: # uniform? no
   w=np.full(len(b),1/len(b)); s= bmax? not
else:
   z = tau*(b-bmax)
   zmax=z.max()
   e=np.exp(z-zmax)
   ess=e.sum()
   if ess<=0: e[np.argmax(b)]=1; ess=1
   w=e/ess
   logsum = zmax + np.log(ess)
   s=(bmax + logsum)/tau
return s,w,b,bmax
```
If tau huge, z-zmax may underflow all but max; ess ≥1. Good.

7. `weighted_gradient(w, a)`:
```
def cross_corr_weights(w, a):
    n=len(a); target=3*n-2; size=...; Fw=rfft(w,size); Fa=rfft(a[::-1],size); e=irfft(Fw*Fa,size); return e[n-1:2*n-1]
def weighted_gradient(w,a): return 2.0*cross_corr_weights(w,a)
```

8. L-BFGS global state:


But objective and jac both compute. To reduce double and ensure consistency, we can use a cache with theta hash. Let's implement a lightweight cache:
Global `_LB_CACHE = {'theta': None, 'value': None, 'grad': None, 'token': 0}` and `_LB_TOKEN = 0`.
In `_lb_obj(theta)`:
```
global _LB_TOKEN
_LB_TOKEN += 1
# compute and store
_LB_CACHE = {'theta': theta, 'value': s, 'grad': grad_theta, 'token': _LB_TOKEN}
return s
```
In `_lb_jac(theta)`:
```
# if _LB_CACHE['token']>0 and np.may_share_memory? Id? Use a flag: _LB_CACHE['next_jac'] = True? scipy calls jac after obj with same x. We can set _LB_CACHE['valid'] = True in obj. In jac, if valid and (id(theta) == id(_LB_CACHE['theta']) or np.array_equal(theta, _LB_CACHE['theta'][:4])?) But if theta is same object, id works. If not, we can still use cache if a global `_LB_CACHE['same']` set? We don't know.
```
Simpler: In objective, store `grad`. In jac, just return stored grad, assuming it corresponds to last objective. L-BFGS algorithm: It may call fun and jac multiple times; it should call jac after the immediately preceding fun with same x. It might call fun at new x, then jac at same x. It won't call jac without preceding fun. There is no other thread. So returning last grad is safe if no other code calls obj/jac in between. We can do that! But if L-BFGS calls fun twice before jac? It typically: `f = fun(x)`, then if finite `g = fprime(x)`. It may call fun for initial x, then line search calls fun multiple times, then after line search at x accepts, it calls fprime(x). The last fun before fprime is at x (accepted). So yes, last cached grad corresponds to current x? In line search, it calls fun for trial points; last call before fprime might be at selected trial x. Good. If it calls fprime after final evaluate, last fun at final x. So returning cached grad is safe if L-BFGS doesn't call fprime for a different x than last fun. I think standard. To be safer, store x and compare first few entries. But if different, return recomputed. Implement:
```
_LB_CACHE = {'x': None, 'grad': None, 'xid': None}
def _lb_obj(theta):
   compute grad_theta
   # store a small hash: first 5 and last 5 and length
   _LB_CACHE['x'] = theta
   _LB_CACHE['hash'] = (len(theta), float(theta[0]), float(theta[-1]), float(np.sum(theta[:16])))
   _LB_CACHE['grad'] = grad_theta
   return s
def _lb_jac(theta):
   if _LB_CACHE['hash'] is not None and len(theta)==... and abs(theta[0]-hash[1])<1e-15 ...:
      return _LB_CACHE['grad']
   else compute fresh and return (no store)
```
Hash with sum first 16 O(16). Good enough. But theta may have small changes? If it's supposed same, exact same floats. Use tuple. Fine.

Need global variables mutable; can assign to `_LB_CACHE['...']`.

`_lb_obj` function top-level:
```
def _lb_obj(theta):
    global _LB_STATE
    tau=_LB_STATE['tau']
    a=softmax(theta)
    n=len(a)
    b=fast_convolve(a)
    bmax=float(b.max())
    z=tau*(b-bmax)
    zmax=float(z.max())
    e=np.exp(z-zmax)
    ess=float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ess) or ess <=0:
       e=np.zeros_like(z); e[np.argmax(b)] = 1.0; ess=1.0
    w=e/ess
    logsum=zmax+np.log(ess)
    s=(bmax+logsum)/tau
    grad_a=2.0*cross_corr_weights(w,a)
    grad_theta=a*(grad_a - float(np.dot(a,grad_a)))
    # sanitize
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_theta)):
       grad_theta=np.zeros_like(theta)
    _LB_CACHE['hash']=(theta.size, float(np.sum(theta[:8])) if theta.size>=8 else 0.0, float(theta[0]) if theta.size else 0.0, float(theta[-1]) if theta.size else 0.0)
    _LB_CACHE['grad']=grad_theta
    return float(s)
```
If theta has nan? L-BFGS may produce? If not finite, return large. Check at top:
```
if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)): return 1e100
```

`exact_proj_grad(a0, time_limit, verbose=False)`:
```
a=normalize_nonneg(a0); n=len(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_s=score(a); t_end=time.time()+time_limit
it=0; no_improve=0; lr=0.2; fr=0.2
while time.time()<t_end:
   it+=1
   b=fast_convolve(a); M=b.max(); cur_s=2*n*M
   update
   temp=max(M*fr,1e-300)
   z=(b-M)/temp; z-=z.max(); e=exp(z); ess=sum; if ess<=0: top=argmax; e=... else w=e/ess
   grad=2*cross(w,a)
   gscale=np.sqrt(np.mean(grad*grad))
   if gscale < 1e-14: break
   # scale step to have direction comparable to a
   # If grad entries ~M? gscale maybe? For uniform, weights average, grad ~? Let's normalize: d_raw = - grad / gscale. Then v=a - lr*d_raw? But since L2 norm of d_raw=√n, lr maybe small. Alternatively use -grad/gscale, then step size in a units lr/sqrt(n)? Need line search handles.
   # Use v = a - lr * grad / max(gscale, eps). Then projected.
   v = a - lr * (grad / gscale)
   p = simplex_project(v)
   d = p - a
   maxd = np.max(np.abs(d))
   if maxd < 1e-15:
       # try pure negative gradient without projection? Already projection. reduce lr
       lr *= 0.5; no_improve +=1
       if lr<1e-8: break
       continue
   # line search over t
   cand_ts = [1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.4, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625]
   # maybe include current best_t from previous
   best_t=0; best_at=None; best_ts=cur_s
   for t in cand_ts:
      if t<=0: continue
      a_t = a + t*d
      minv=np.min(a_t)
      if minv < -1e-9: continue
      a_t = np.maximum(a_t,0.0)
      ss=a_t.sum()
      if ss < 0.01: continue
      a_t = a_t/ss
      b_t=fast_convolve(a_t); sc=2*n*b_t.max()
      if sc < best_ts - 1e-14 or (abs(sc-best_ts)<=1e-14 and t>best_t):
          best_ts=sc; best_t=t; best_at=a_t
   if best_t>0:
      a=best_at.copy()
      if best_ts < best_s - 1e-14:
          best_s=best_ts; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
          if verbose and it%100: print
          lr = min(0.5, lr*1.05)
      else:
          no_improve +=1
          # no exact improvement but maybe soft? accept? If accepted local but not global best, could still be beneficial for future. We can accept if cur? Actually a changed; even if exact score not lower than global best, it might help escape. We should allow accept if best_ts < cur_s? Since line search ensures <=cur_s? It may choose t with score lower than current a, but not lower than global best. So accept for exploration. no_improve counts relative to global best, but reset when global improves. We should not reset lr based only global.
          if best_t < 0.3: lr *=0.8
   else:
      no_improve +=1
      lr *=0.5
   # adapt fr: if no_improve high, smooth by increasing fr; if improving for many, sharpen
   if no_improve > 30:
       if no_improve % 30 ==0:
          fr = min(1.0, fr*1.5)
       if lr < 1e-6: break
   else no_improve low:
       fr = max(0.05, fr*0.98)
```

`fourier_init(n, rng, Kmax, amp)`:
```
theta = np.zeros(n)
K = int(rng.integers(2, Kmax+1))
for m in 1..K:
   amp_m = amp * rng.uniform(0.2,1.0) / np.sqrt(m)
   ph = rng.uniform(0,2π)
   theta += amp_m * np.cos(2π m i/n + ph)
# maybe add linear drift? scale invariant; but edge asymmetry via sine included by phase.
# optionally add small Gaussian
theta += rng.standard_normal(n)*amp*0.1
theta -= theta.mean()
return softmax(theta)
```

Let's draft code structure:

```
"""
Hybrid L-BFGS (softmax parameterization, soft-max surrogate) plus exact projected
gradient line-search to minimize 2n max(autoconv)/sum^2.
Searches several lengths (1024, 2048, 3072) from parent and Fourier/random inits.
"""
import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

_LB_STATE = {'tau': 1.0}
_LB_CACHE = {'hash': None, 'grad': None}

def _fft_size(target):
    if target <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (target - 1).bit_length()

def fast_convolve(a):
    n = len(a)
    size = _fft_size(2*n - 1)
    F = rfft(a, size)
    return irfft(F*F, size)[:2*n-1]

...
```

L-BFGS functions:
```
def softmax(theta):
    theta = np.asarray(theta)
    theta = theta - np.max(theta)
    e = np.exp(theta)
    s = e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
       # fallback uniform; set max to 1
       idx = np.argmax(theta)
       e = np.zeros_like(theta); e[idx]=1; s=1
    return e/s
```
If theta contains -inf (log zero) fine. If +inf? not.

`x_to_theta(x)`:
```
n = len(x)+1
theta = np.empty(n)
theta[:-1] = x
theta[-1] = -np.sum(x) / n
return theta
```
If x large, sum could lose precision but okay. If not finite return None.

`_soft_from_theta(theta, tau)` returns s, w, a, grad_full.
```
a=softmax(theta)
n=len(a)
b=fast_convolve(a)
bmax=float(b.max())
if bmax <= 0: ...
z=tau*(b-bmax)
zmax=float(z.max())
e=np.exp(z-zmax)
ess=float(e.sum())
if not finite/<=0: e=zeros; e[argmax(b)]=1; ess=1
w=e/ess
logsum=zmax+np.log(ess)
s=(bmax+logsum)/tau
grad_a=2.0*cross_corr_weights(w,a)
grad_full=a*(grad_a - float(np.dot(a,grad_a)))
grad_full -= float(grad_full.mean())
return s,w,b,bmax,grad_full
```
If s not finite, return large.

```
# sanitize
if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): grad=zeros
_LB_CACHE['hash']=(x.size, float(x[0]) if x.size else 0.0, float(x[-1]) if x.size else 0.0, float(np.sum(x[:8])) if x.size else 0.0)
```

Now exact_proj_grad implementation detail with b maintenance:
```
def exact_proj_grad(a0, time_limit, rng, verbose=False):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a0).copy()
    n = len(a)
    b = fast_convolve(a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)  # maybe
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_s = 2.0*n*float(b.max())
    t_end = time.monotonic() + time_limit
    lr = 1.0
    fr = 0.2
    it=0; no_improve=0; streak_improve=0
    recompute_next=False
    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
       it += 1
       if recompute_next or it % 200 == 0 or b.min() < -1e-8:
           b = fast_convolve(a); b=np.maximum(b,0)
           recompute_next=False
       M = float(b.max())
       cur_s = 2.0*n*M
       if cur_s < best_s - 1e-14:
           best_s=cur_s; best_a=a.copy(); streak_improve +=1
           # if best improved, sharpen fr
           fr = max(0.05, fr*0.97)
       else:
           no_improve +=1
           streak_improve = 0
           if no_improve >= 25 and no_improve % 25 ==0:
               fr = min(1.0, fr*1.6)
           if no_improve >= 60:
               # random perturbation
               if rng.random() < 0.4:
                   sigma = 0.05 * np.linalg.norm(a)
                   a_p = normalize_nonneg(a + rng.standard_normal(n)*sigma)
                   b_p = fast_convolve(a_p); sc = 2*n*max
                   if sc < best_s * 1.02: # allow slight worsening
                       a = a_p; b = b_p; cur_s=sc
                       no_improve = 0
                       if verbose: print perturb
               if no_improve > 200:
                   break
       if M <= 0: break
       w = make_weights(b, fr)
       grad = 2.0*cross_corr_weights(w, a)
       gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
       if gnorm < 1e-14: break
       g = grad / gnorm
       step = lr * float(np.linalg.norm(a))
       v = a - step * g
       p = simplex_project(v)
       d = p - a
       if float(np.max(np.abs(d))) < 1e-15:
           lr *= 0.5
           no_improve +=1
           if lr < 1e-8: break
           continue
       # precompute conv a,d and d,d
       cd = fast_convolve(a, d)
       dd = fast_convolve(d, d)
       # candidates
       ts = [1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.4, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625]
       best_t = 0.0; best_sc = cur_s; best_b_t = None
       for tval in ts:
           # b_t = b + 2t cd + t^2 dd
           bt = b + (2.0*tval)*cd + (tval*tval)*dd
           # max; if numerical small negative, no issue. Maybe clip? For max positive.
           m = float(np.max(bt))
           sc = 2.0*n*m
           if sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
               best_sc = sc; best_t = tval; best_b_t = bt
       if best_t > 0:
           # accept
           a = a + best_t * d
           # due round: ensure nonneg and sum
           if np.min(a) < -1e-12:
               a = np.maximum(a,0)
               ss=a.sum(); a/=ss
               b = fast_convolve(a)
           else:
               ss = a.sum()
               if abs(ss-1.0) > 1e-10:
                   a = a/ss
                   b = fast_convolve(a)
               else:
                   b = best_b_t.copy()
                   if b.min() < -1e-8:
                       b = np.maximum(b,0.0)
           if best_sc < cur_s - 1e-14:
               lr = min(3.0, lr*1.05)
           else:
               lr = max(1e-4, lr*0.7)
       else:
           no_improve +=1
           lr = max(1e-4, lr*0.6)
       if verbose and it % 200 ==0: print best_s
       if best_s < 1.5027? no, can continue.
    # final recompute best from best_a
    best_sc = float(evaluate_sequence? internal)
    return best_a, best_s
```

Let's verify mathematical gradient for L-BFGS soft objective. We use f=s (not 2n*s), gradient wrt θ = L. The soft s = (1/τ)logΣ exp(τ b_k). ∂s/∂a = Σ w_k ∂b_k/∂a. ∂b_k/∂a_i = 2 a_{k-i} if valid. Thus grad_a = 2 cross_corr_weights(w,a) as implemented. Good. Then chain to θ: a_i=softmax(θ_i) for full θ with constant arbitrary. ∂a_j/∂θ_i = a_i(δ_{ij}-a_j). ∂s/∂θ_i = Σ_j ∂s/∂a_j a_i(δ-a_j)=a_i(grad_a_i - a·grad_a). Good. For mean-zero x mapping L, grad_x = L.T grad_full? Actually θ = Lx, ∂θ_j/∂x_i = L_{j,i}. grad_x_i = Σ_j grad_full_j L_{j,i}. L first rows identity; last row -1/n. So grad_x_i = grad_full_i - (1/n) grad_full_last. We use that. Good.

For Fourier x: θ = B x where B shape (2K,n) with rows 2cos etc, i.e., θ_i = Σ_p B_{p,i} x_p. Then grad_x_p = Σ_i B_{p,i} grad_epsi_i. We use `basis.T @ grad_full` (B columns i). Good.

Now, `cross_corr_weights` correctness with w normalized sum1. For b_k = a^T Q_k a, gradient = 2Q_k a. Weighted sum gradient =2Σ w_k Q_k a. Q_k a_i = a_{k-i} if valid. So component i =2Σ_{j} w_{i+j} a_j. Our cross returns c_i =Σ_j w_{i+j} a_j via conv(w, a_rev)[n-1+i]. Let's recheck with n=3. w length5, a length3, a_rev [a2,a1,a0]. conv e length7:
e0=w0 a2
e1=w0 a1 + w1 a2
e2=w0 a0 + w1 a1 + w2 a2
e3=w1 a0 + w2 a1 + w3 a2
e4=w2 a0 + w3 a1 + w4 a2
e5=w3 a0 + w4 a1
e6=w4 a0
Desired i=0: j=0..2 w_j a_j? Σ_j w_{0+j} a_j = w0 a0 + w1 a1 + w2 a2 = e2. n-1+i =2+0=2. i=1: w1 a0+w2 a1+w3 a2=e3. i=2: w2a0+w3a1+w4a2=e4. Good.

Now, `fast_convolve(a,d)` for d can be non-integer? fine. If d all zeros, returns zeros. If size maybe target 2n-1. Use `_fft_size`.

Potential numerical issue in exact b update: `bt = b + 2t cd + t^2 dd`. Since b,cd,dd from FFT may have roundoff. For t=1 and p from projection, a_new should be exactly p; b_new from formula might differ from fast_convolve(p) by ~1e-15. We could if t==1 set b=fast_convolve(p) to ensure? t often 1. But formula fast enough. To avoid drift over many accepted t, recompute every 200. Good. Maybe after accept with t=1, use `b = fast_convolve(a)` if it%5==0? More safety. Since exact gradient can be hurt by drift. We can recompute every 50 accepted. 0.05s*2000=10s, okay. Let's recompute every 50 or if `np.max(np.abs(b - expected? no))`.

Line search candidate t=1 gives best if direction reduces. Good.

Now, exact `simplex_project(v)` with v maybe negative large. It sorts O(n log n) each iter. n=2048, 2000 iters fine. Could optimize with `np.sort` C.

For exact step, we use `g = grad / gnorm`, step = lr*norma. If gnorm computed from grad of soft weights; if weights very sharp single peak, grad = 2 shifted a, gnorm=2*||a||. Then step=lr*||a||, g is unit; v = a - lr*||a|| * unit. Magnitude step = lr||a||. If lr=1, v moves by L2 distance equal to ||a||. Could be large but line search. Good. Maybe start lr=0.5 to be safe. Let's start lr=0.7.

Adapt lr: If selected t=1 and score improvement, lr *=1.1 up to 5. If t small, reduce. If no accept, reduce. We need `best_t` maybe 0.8. Use if best_t >=0.5 increase.

`no_improve` and perturb: If no global improvement for 100 iters, increase fr. If no accept for 50, perturb. Let's code robust.

Pseudo exact more concrete:
```
def exact_proj_grad(a0, time_limit, rng, verbose=False):
    a=normalize_nonneg(a0)
    n=len(a)
    b=fast_convolve(a); b=np.maximum(b,0)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_s=score(b,n)
    t_end=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    lr=0.7; fr=0.2; it=0; no_improve=0; no_accept=0; last_best_it=0
    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
       it+=1
       if it % 50 ==0:
          b=fast_convolve(a); b=np.maximum(b,0)
       M=float(b.max()); cur_s=2*n*M
       if cur_s < best_s - 1e-14:
          best_s=cur_s; best_a=a.copy(); last_best_it=it; no_improve=0; no_accept=0
          if verbose...
       else:
          no_improve +=1
       if M <=0: break
       w=make_weights(b, fr)
       grad=2*cross_corr_weights(w,a)
       gnorm=norm(grad)
       if gnorm <1e-14:
          # no gradient, break or perturb
          no_accept +=1
          if no_accept>10: break
          continue
       g=grad/gnorm
       step=lr*norm(a)
       v=a - step*g
       p=simplex_project(v)
       d=p-a
       maxd=max(abs(d))
       if maxd <1e-15:
          lr *=0.5; no_accept+=1; if lr<1e-6 break; continue
       cd=fast_convolve(a,d); dd=fast_convolve(d,d)
       # candidate ts, maybe include previous best last_t
       ts = [1.0, 0.9, 0.75, 0.6, 0.45, 0.3, 0.18, 0.1]
       best_t=0; best_sc=cur_s; best_bt=None
       for tv in ts:
          bt = b + 2*tv*cd + tv*tv*dd
          # If due round negative, max still
          sc=2*n*float(bt.max())
          if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
             best_sc=sc; best_t=tv; best_bt=bt
       if best_t >0:
          a = a + best_t*d
          # ensure
          if np.min(a) < -1e-10:
             a=np.maximum(a,0); a/=a.sum(); b=fast_convolve(a); b=np.maximum(b,0)
          else:
             ss=a.sum()
             if abs(ss-1)>1e-10:
                a/=ss; b=fast_convolve(a); b=np.maximum(b,0)
             else:
                b=best_bt
                if b.min() < -1e-8: b=np.maximum(b,0)
          # lr adapt
          if best_t >= 0.5:
             lr = min(4.0, lr*1.08)
          else:
             lr = max(1e-4, lr*0.75)
          no_accept=0
          # update global if improved
          # (done at top next loop, but also if best_sc < best_s then could set now to avoid relying on top with recomputed b)
          if best_sc < best_s:
             best_s=best_sc; best_a=a.copy()
       else:
          no_accept +=1
          lr = max(1e-4, lr*0.6)
          if no_accept > 30:
             # random perturb
             sigma=0.08*norm(a)
             a=normalize_nonneg(a + rng.standard_normal(n)*sigma)
             b=fast_convolve(a); b=np.maximum(b,0)
             cur_s=2n*max
             # if perturbation worse than best*1.05, revert to best
             if cur_s > best_s*1.05:
                a=best_a.copy(); b=fast_convolve(a); b=np.maximum(b,0)
             no_accept=0; lr=0.7; fr=min(1.0,fr*1.5)
       # adapt fr and no_improve
       if no_improve > 40:
          # smooth
          if no_improve % 40 ==0:
              fr = min(1.0, fr*1.4)
              lr = max(0.2, lr*0.8)
       else:
          if no_improve < 10:
              fr = max(0.05, fr*0.98)
       if no_improve > 300:
          # try larger perturbation / break
          if rng.random()<0.5:
             a=best_a.copy(); b=fast_convolve(a); no_improve=0; fr=0.5; lr=0.7
          else:
             break
    # final
    b=fast_convolve(best_a); best_s=2n*max
    return best_a,best_s
```
This seems okay.

Potential performance: In line search, `bt = b + 2*tv*cd + tv*tv*dd` allocates array each candidate (8*2047) ok. Could precompute but fine.

```
def lbfgs_refine(a0, fracs, maxiter_per_tau, label=''):
    a0=normalize_nonneg(a0)
    n=len(a0)
    best_a=a0; best_s=score
    theta=np.log(np.maximum(a0,1e-12)); theta-=theta.mean(); x0=theta[:-1]; best_x=x0.copy(); x_cur=x0.copy()
    for fr in fracs:
       # compute score current
       cur_a=x_to_a(x_cur); cur_s=score(cur_a)
       if cur_s < best_s: update
       # tau based on cur_a
       ...
       res=minimize(...)
       x_trial=res.x if finite else x_cur
       # evaluate trial
       trial_a=x_to_a(x_trial); trial_s=score(trial_a)
       if trial_s < best_s:
           best_s=trial_s; best_a=trial_a; best_x=x_trial.copy()
       # choose next x: if trial_s <= cur_s*1.002? use trial else best_x
       if trial_s <= cur_s * 1.002 and trial_s <= score? maybe:
           x_cur = x_trial
       else:
           x_cur = best_x.copy()
       # if no improvement after this tau, maybe try x_trial? We'll proceed.
    # final exact from best_x
    return best_a,best_s
```

```

x=np.zeros(2K); basis=prepare? To avoid recompute, pass basis. For p in rows: x[p]=dot(theta, basis[p])/(2*n) ? Norm squared for row 2cos: if m not n/2, norm=2*n? `fourier_family_refine(n, maxstarts, fracs, maxiter, rng, parent_a=None)`:
```
```
basis=prepare_basis(n,K)
_FC_STATE['basis']=basis
best_a=None; best_s=inf
numcomponents=2*K
for s in range(maxstarts):
   if s==0:
      if parent_a is not None:
         theta = log(parent_a)-mean; x0=theta_to_fourier_x(theta,basis)
      else: x0=zeros
   else:
      # random: mix of parent coefs and random
      scale = 0.8 * (1.0/(s+1)? maybe)
      x0 = rng.normal(size=numcomponents) * (1.0 + 0.2*np.sqrt(s)?).limit 1.5
      # for early starts, small scale; later larger for diversity
   x_cur=x0.copy()
   start_score = score(softmax(x_cur@basis))
   for fr in fracs:
      a=softmax(x_cur@basis); M=...; tau...
      _FC_STATE['tau']=tau
      res=minimize(_fc_obj, x_cur, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=_fc_jac, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-8})
      if finite: x_trial=res.x else x_cur
      a_trial=softmax(x_trial@basis); s_trial=score
      if s_trial<best_s update best_x/a
      # choose next x similarly
      a_cur=softmax(x_cur@basis); s_cur=score
      if s_trial <= s_cur*1.005: x_cur=x_trial else x_cur=best_x(x? need store)
   # after fracs, evaluate best_x
```
Need store best_x_global. Since coefficients length small, easy.

Let's plan propose_candidate step-by-step:
```
def propose_candidate():
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+1780
    rng=np.random.default_rng(20240527)
    best_seq=None; best_score=inf
    # helper update_func but no closures: define top-level update? We can just inline.
    parent = get_parent()
```

```
def _soft_from_theta(theta, tau):
    if not finite theta or tau<=0: return inf, zeros
    a=softmax(theta); n=len(a); b=fast_convolve(a); bmax=...
    if bmax<=0: ...
    z=tau*(b-bmax); zmax; e; ess; if invalid...
    w=e/ess; s=(bmax+zmax+log(ess))/tau
    if not finite(s): return inf, zeros
    grad_a=2*cross_corr_weights(w,a)
    grad=a*(grad_a - dot(a,grad_a)); grad-=mean
    if not finite grad: grad=zeros
    return s, grad
```

Now, exact_proj_grad uses `make_weights(b, fr)`. Need top-level.

`make_weights`:
```
def make_weights(b, fr):
    M=float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0:
        return np.full(len(b), 1.0/len(b))
    temp = max(M*fr, 1e-300)
    z = (b - M) / temp
    zmax = float(z.max())
    e = np.exp(z - zmax)
    ess = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ess) or ess <= 0:
        e = np.zeros_like(b); e[np.argmax(b)] = 1.0; ess = 1.0
    return e / ess
```
Since b-M max 0, zmax 0; but if numerical, okay. If fr=0, temp=eps.

`random_fourier_init`:
```
def random_fourier_init(n, rng, Kmax=12, amp=1.0, noise=0.0):
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
    theta=np.zeros(n)
    K=int(rng.integers(3, Kmax+1))
    for m in range(1,K+1):
       a_m=amp * rng.uniform(0.2,1.0) / np.sqrt(float(m))
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       theta += a_m * np.cos(2*np.pi*m*i/n + ph)
    if noise>0: theta += noise*rng.standard_normal(n)
    theta -= theta.mean()
    return softmax(theta)
```
No lambda. `rng.integers(3,Kmax+1)` returns int.

`smooth_parent`:
Use FFT from `np.fft`.
```
def smooth_parent(a, rng, K=64, noise=0.02):
    theta = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12)); theta -= theta.mean()
    F = np.fft.fft(theta)
    n=len(F)
    mmax=min(K, n//2)
    mask = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
    if mmax>0:
       mask[1:mmax+1]=True
       start=n-mmax
       mask[start:n]=True
    F_low = F*mask
    theta_low = np.fft.ifft(F_low).real
    if noise>0: theta_low += noise*rng.standard_normal(n)
    theta_low -= theta_low.mean()
    return softmax(theta_low)
```
This discards DC (mean already zero). Good.

`noise_perturb`:
```
def noise_perturb(a, rng, sigma=0.02):
    return normalize_nonneg(a + sigma*norm(a)*rng.standard_normal(a.size))
```
Maybe add.

`interpolate_seq`: previous.

`fourier_basis(n,K)`:
```
i=np.arange(n)
rows=[]
for m in range(1,K+1):
    w=2*np.pi*m*i/n
    rows.append(2*np.cos(w))
    rows.append(-2*np.sin(w))
return np.vstack(rows)
```

`theta_to_fourier_x(theta, basis)`:
```
n=len(theta); K=basis.shape[0]//2; x=np.zeros(2K)
for p in range(2K): x[p] = float(np.dot(basis[p], theta) / (2.0*n))
return x
```
Because row norm maybe 2n (for Dirichlet exact if n even and m not n/2; for n small okay). Good.

`fourier_family_refine(n,K,maxstarts,fracs,maxiter,rng,parent_a=None)`:
Need global `_FC_BASIS`. Set before. If K too small maybe 4. If maxstarts 0 return None, inf.
```
def fourier_family_refine(n, K, maxstarts, fracs, maxiter, rng, parent_a=None):
    global _FC_BASIS, _FC_TAU
    basis=fourier_basis(n,K); _FC_BASIS=basis
    best_a=None; best_s=inf; best_x=None
    comp=basis.shape[0]
    # parent_x if parent_a
    parent_x=None
    if parent_a is not None:
        pa=normalize_nonneg(parent_a)
        th=np.log(np.maximum(pa,1e-12)); th-=th.mean()
        parent_x=theta_to_fourier_x(th,basis)
    for st in range(maxstarts):
        x_cur=np.zeros(comp)
        if st==0 and parent_x is not None:
            x_cur=parent_x.copy()
        elif parent_x is not None:
            x_cur=parent_x + 0.5*rng.normal(size=comp)
        else:
            x_cur=rng.normal(size=comp) * (0.5 + 0.1*st)
        # maybe scale by 1
        for fr in fracs:
            a_cur=softmax(x_cur @ basis)
            b=fast_convolve(a_cur); M=float(b.max()); s_cur=2*n*M
            if s_cur < best_s:
                best_s=s_cur; best_a=a_cur.copy(); best_x=x_cur.copy()
            if M <=0: break
            _FC_TAU=min(1/(max(M*fr,1e-300)), 1e6)
            try:
                res=minimize(_fc_obj, x_cur.copy(), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=_fc_jac,
                             options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-14, 'gtol':1e-8, 'maxls':20, 'disp':False})
                x_trial = res.x if np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)) else x_cur
            except Exception:
                x_trial = x_cur
            a_trial=softmax(x_trial @ basis); s_trial=2*n*float(fast_convolve(a_trial).max())
            if s_trial < best_s:
                best_s=s_trial; best_a=a_trial.copy(); best_x=x_trial.copy()
            # choose next
            if s_trial <= s_cur * 1.003:
                x_cur = x_trial
            elif best_x is not None:
                x_cur = best_x.copy()
        # after fracs, evaluate best_x too
    if best_a is None:
        # fallback from parent or uniform
        if parent_a is not None: return normalize_nonneg(parent_a), score_norm(parent_a)
        a=np.ones(n)/n; return a, 2.0
    return best_a, best_s
```
But `best_a` a normalized from softmax; sum1. Good. `score_norm` calls evaluate? internal.

Time: In inner loop, `fast_convolve` for current and trial; L-BFGS objective also computes many. Could be okay. We don't use current exact for tau if `M` from current. Good.

```
def lbfgs_refine(a0, fracs, maxiter_per_tau, label=''):
    global _LB_TAU
    a0=normalize_nonneg(a0)
    n=len(a0)
    if n==1: return a0, 2.0
    a_cur=a0.copy(); best_a=a0.copy(); best_s=score_norm(a0)
    th=np.log(np.maximum(a_cur,1e-12)); th-=th.mean(); x_cur=th[:-1].copy(); best_x=x_cur.copy()
    for fr in fracs:
        cur_a=x_to_a(x_cur); cur_s=score_norm(cur_a)
        if cur_s<best_s: update
        b=fast_convolve(cur_a); M=float(b.max())
        _LB_TAU=min(1/(max(M*fr,1e-300)),1e6)
        try: res=minimize(...)
        if finite x_trial else x_cur
        trial_a=x_to_a(x_trial); trial_s=score_norm(trial_a)
        if trial_s<best_s: update best_x
        if trial_s <= cur_s*1.003:
            x_cur=x_trial
        elif best_x is not None:
            x_cur=best_x.copy()
    # final evaluate best_a
    return best_a,best_s
```

```
def fast_convolve(a, b=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if b is None: b=a
    else: b=np.asarray(b,dtype=np.float64)
    n=len(a)
    m=len(b)
    target=n+m-1
    size=_fft_size(target)
    Fa=rfft(a,size); Fb=rfft(b,size); return irfft(Fa*Fb,size)[:target]
```
This supports cross. Previously only auto; but for exact and grad cross uses separate. For auto, b=None. Good. For `fast_convolve(a,d)`, target 2n-1. For `fast_convolve(d,d)`, okay. `x_to_theta`:
```
def x_to_theta(x):
    n=len(x)+1
    theta=np.empty(n,dtype=np.float64)
    if n>1:
       theta[:-1]=x
       theta[-1] = -np.sum(x)
    else:
       theta[0]=0.0
    return theta
```
`_soft_meanzero`:
```
grad_x = grad[:-1] - grad[-1]
```

```
    n=_LB_N
    if _LB_NO_REF is None or n==0: return 1e100
    raw=np.zeros(n)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_x)): grad_x=np.zeros_like(x)
    return float(2.0*n*s)
```

```
def lbfgs_refine(a0, fracs, maxiter_per_tau, label=''):
    global _LB_N, _LB_REF, _LB_NO_REF, _LB_TAU
    a0=normalize_nonneg(a0); n=len(a0)
    if n <=1: return a0, 2.0*n*float(np.max(fast_convolve(a0)))? score_norm.
    ref=int(np.argmax(a0))
    _LB_N=n; _LB_REF=ref
    idx=np.arange(n); _LB_NO_REF=idx[idx != ref]
    a_cur=a0.copy(); best_a=a0.copy(); best_s=score_norm(a0); best_x=None
    loga=np.log(np.maximum(a_cur,1e-15)); raw=loga - loga[ref]; x_cur=np.clip(raw[_LB_NO_REF], -30,30)
    best_x=x_cur.copy()
    for fr in fracs:
       cur_a=x_to_a_ref(x_cur); cur_s=score_norm(cur_a)
       if cur_s<best_s: update best_x
       b=fast_convolve(cur_a); M=...
       _LB_TAU=...
       res=minimize(_lb_obj, x_cur.copy(), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=_lb_jac, options=...)
       if finite: x_trial=np.clip(res.x,-30,30) else x_cur
       trial_a=x_to_a_ref(x_trial); trial_s=score_norm(trial_a)
       update
       if trial_s <= cur_s*1.003: x_cur=x_trial else x_cur=best_x.copy()
    return best
```

```
def phase_preset_init(n, rng):
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
    theta = 0.9*np.cos(2*np.pi*i/n + np.pi/4)
    theta += 0.4*np.cos(4*np.pi*i/n + 3*np.pi/4)
    theta += 0.2*np.cos(6*np.pi*i/n + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
    theta += 0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)
    theta -= theta.mean()
    return softmax(theta)
```
Add to seeds. Good.

Maybe amplitude 0.9 phase π/4 not enough but L-BFGS can refine.

```
seeds.append(phase_preset_init(L, rng))
```

Now, one subtlety: In L-BFGS reference parameterization, raw_ref=0, x variables can be clipped. But `raw - mean(raw)` has mean zero. The objective is invariant to constant shift, but this parameterization maps x uniquely. Gradient wrt x = grad_full at x positions only if grad_full sum zero. We enforce grad_full -= mean. Good.
```python
"""
Hybrid search for minimizing 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2:
1. Deterministic/random low-dimensional Fourier L-BFGS seeds.
2. Full-sequence L-BFGS in an anchored softmax parameterization, using
   a soft-max surrogate of the peak convolution and annealed temperature.
3. Exact projected-gradient line search with FFT-based incremental convolution.
Several lengths (about 1024-4096) are explored, starting from the parent
when available, plus Fourier/random perturbations.
"""

import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

_LB_N = 0
_LB_REF = 0
_LB_NO_REF = None
_LB_TAU = 1.0

_FC_BASIS = None
_FC_TAU = 1.0


def _fft_size(target):
    if target <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (target - 1).bit_length()


def fast_convolve(a, b=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if b is None:
        target = 2 * n - 1
        size = _fft_size(target)
        fa = rfft(a, size)
        return irfft(fa * fa, size)[:target]
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    target = n + len(b) - 1
    size = _fft_size(target)
    fa = rfft(a, size)
    fb = rfft(b, size)
    return irfft(fa * fb, size)[:target]


def normalize_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a[a < 0.0] = 0.0
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(len(a), dtype=np.float64) / max(len(a), 1)
    return a / s


def score_norm(a):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    b = fast_convolve(a)
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return 2.0 * n * m


def softmax(theta):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    if theta.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
        return np.ones(theta.size) / theta.size
    z = theta - float(np.max(theta))
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        e = np.zeros_like(theta)
        e[int(np.argmax(theta))] = 1.0
        s = 1.0
    return e / s


def simplex_project(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(v)
    if n == 0:
        return v
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    cond = u * np.arange(1, n + 1) > (cssv - 1.0)
    if cond.any():
        rho = int(np.nonzero(cond)[0][-1])
        theta = (float(cssv[rho]) - 1.0) / float(rho + 1)
        out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    else:
        out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return out / s


def cross_corr_weights(w, a):
    n = len(a)
    e = fast_convolve(w, a[::-1])
    return e[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def _soft_from_theta(theta, tau):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(theta)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
        return np.inf, np.zeros(n)
    if tau <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(tau):
        tau = 1.0

    a = softmax(theta)
    b = fast_convolve(a)
    bmax = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(bmax) or bmax <= 0.0:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(n)

    z = tau * (b - bmax)
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z - zmax)
    ess = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(ess) or ess <= 0.0:
        e = np.zeros_like(z)
        e[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        ess = 1.0
    w = e / ess
    logsum = zmax + float(np.log(ess))
    s = (bmax + logsum) / tau
    if not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.inf, np.zeros(n)

    grad_a = 2.0 * cross_corr_weights(w, a)
    grad = a * (grad_a - float(np.dot(a, grad_a)))
    grad -= float(np.mean(grad))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(n)
    return s, grad


# ---- Full-sequence L-BFGS in anchored softmax parameterization ----

def _lb_obj(x):
    n = _LB_N
    if n == 0 or _LB_NO_REF is None:
        return 1e100
    raw = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if len(x) == len(_LB_NO_REF):
        raw[_LB_NO_REF] = x
    raw[_LB_REF] = 0.0
    theta = raw - float(np.mean(raw))
    s, grad = _soft_from_theta(theta, _LB_TAU)
    if not np.isfinite(s):
        return 1e100
    gx = grad[_LB_NO_REF].copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gx)):
        gx = np.zeros_like(x)
    return float(2.0 * n * s)


def _lb_jac(x):
    n = _LB_N
    if n == 0 or _LB_NO_REF is None:
        return np.zeros_like(x)
    raw = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if len(x) == len(_LB_NO_REF):
        raw[_LB_NO_REF] = x
    raw[_LB_REF] = 0.0
    theta = raw - float(np.mean(raw))
    s, grad = _soft_from_theta(theta, _LB_TAU)
    if not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.zeros_like(x)
    gx = grad[_LB_NO_REF].copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gx)):
        gx = np.zeros_like(x)
    return 2.0 * n * gx


def x_to_a_ref(x):
    n = _LB_N
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(1)
    if _LB_NO_REF is None:
        return np.ones(n) / n
    raw = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if len(x) == len(_LB_NO_REF):
        raw[_LB_NO_REF] = x
    raw[_LB_REF] = 0.0
    theta = raw - float(np.mean(raw))
    return softmax(theta)


def lbfgs_refine(a0, fracs, maxiter_per_tau, label=""):
    global _LB_N, _LB_REF, _LB_NO_REF, _LB_TAU
    a0 = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    n = len(a0)
    if n <= 1:
        return a0, score_norm(a0)

    ref = int(np.argmax(a0))
    _LB_N = n
    _LB_REF = ref
    idx = np.arange(n)
    _LB_NO_REF = idx[idx != ref]

    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_s = float(score_norm(a0))
    best_x = None

    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a0, 1e-15))
    raw0 = loga - loga[ref]
    x_cur = np.clip(raw0[_LB_NO_REF], -30.0, 30.0).astype(np.float64)
    best_x = x_cur.copy()

    opt = {
        "maxiter": int(maxiter_per_tau),
        "ftol": 1e-12,
        "gtol": 1e-8,
        "maxls": 12,
        "disp": False,
    }

    for fr in fracs:
        cur_a = x_to_a_ref(x_cur)
        cur_s = float(score_norm(cur_a))
        if np.isfinite(cur_s) and cur_s < best_s:
            best_s = cur_s
            best_a = cur_a.copy()
            best_x = x_cur.copy()

        b = fast_convolve(cur_a)
        m = float(np.max(b))
        if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
            break
        _LB_TAU = min(1.0 / max(m * float(fr), 1e-300), 1e6)

        try:
            init_val = _lb_obj(x_cur)
            if not np.isfinite(init_val):
                continue
            res = minimize(_lb_obj, x_cur.copy(), method="L-BFGS-B",
                           jac=_lb_jac, options=opt)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                x_trial = np.clip(res.x, -30.0, 30.0).astype(np.float64)
            else:
                x_trial = x_cur.copy()
        except Exception:
            x_trial = x_cur.copy()

        trial_a = x_to_a_ref(x_trial)
        trial_s = float(score_norm(trial_a))
        if np.isfinite(trial_s) and trial_s < best_s:
            best_s = trial_s
            best_a = trial_a.copy()
            best_x = x_trial.copy()

        if np.isfinite(trial_s) and trial_s <= cur_s * 1.003:
            x_cur = x_trial
        else:
            if best_x is not None:
                x_cur = best_x.copy()

        if label:
            print(f"      L-BFGS {label} fr={fr:.3f} best={best_s:.6f}")

    return best_a, best_s


# ---- Low-dimensional Fourier parameterized L-BFGS ----

def _fc_obj(x):
    if _FC_BASIS is None:
        return 1e100
    theta = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64) @ _FC_BASIS
    s, grad = _soft_from_theta(theta, _FC_TAU)
    if not np.isfinite(s):
        return 1e100
    gx = (_FC_BASIS.T @ grad).copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gx)):
        gx = np.zeros_like(x)
    return float(2.0 * _FC_BASIS.shape[1] * s)


def _fc_jac(x):
    if _FC_BASIS is None:
        return np.zeros_like(x)
    theta = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64) @ _FC_BASIS
    s, grad = _soft_from_theta(theta, _FC_TAU)
    if not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.zeros_like(x)
    gx = (_FC_BASIS.T @ grad).copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gx)):
        gx = np.zeros_like(x)
    return 2.0 * _FC_BASIS.shape[1] * gx


def fourier_basis(n, K):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    rows = []
    for m in range(1, K + 1):
        w = 2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * i / float(n)
        rows.append(2.0 * np.cos(w))
        rows.append(-2.0 * np.sin(w))
    if not rows:
        return np.zeros((0, n), dtype=np.float64)
    return np.vstack(rows).astype(np.float64)


def theta_to_fourier_x(theta, basis):
    n = len(theta)
    x = np.zeros(basis.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
    denom = 2.0 * float(n)
    for p in range(basis.shape[0]):
        x[p] = float(np.dot(basis[p], theta) / denom)
    return x


def fourier_family_refine(n, K, maxstarts, fracs, maxiter, rng, parent_a=None):
    global _FC_BASIS, _FC_TAU
    basis = fourier_basis(n, max(1, int(K)))
    _FC_BASIS = basis
    comp = basis.shape[0]

    best_a = None
    best_s = np.inf
    best_x = None

    parent_x = None
    if parent_a is not None:
        pa = normalize_nonneg(parent_a)
        th = np.log(np.maximum(pa, 1e-15))
        th -= float(np.mean(th))
        parent_x = theta_to_fourier_x(th, basis)

    opt = {
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "ftol": 1e-12,
        "gtol": 1e-8,
        "maxls": 12,
        "disp": False,
    }

    for st in range(max(0, int(maxstarts))):
        x_cur = np.zeros(comp, dtype=np.float64)
        if parent_x is not None:
            if st == 0:
                x_cur = parent_x.copy()
            else:
                scale = 0.25 + 0.10 * float(st)
                x_cur = parent_x + scale * rng.standard_normal(comp)
        else:
            scale = 0.60 + 0.10 * float(st)
            x_cur = scale * rng.standard_normal(comp)
        x_cur = np.clip(x_cur, -8.0, 8.0)

        for fr in fracs:
            a_cur = softmax(x_cur @ basis)
            b_cur = fast_convolve(a_cur)
            m_cur = float(np.max(b_cur))
            s_cur = 2.0 * n * m_cur
            if np.isfinite(s_cur) and s_cur < best_s:
                best_s = s_cur
                best_a = a_cur.copy()
                best_x = x_cur.copy()

            if not np.isfinite(m_cur) or m_cur <= 0.0:
                break
            _FC_TAU = min(1.0 / max(m_cur * float(fr), 1e-300), 1e6)

            try:
                if not np.isfinite(_fc_obj(x_cur)):
                    continue
                res = minimize(_fc_obj, x_cur.copy(), method="L-BFGS-B",
                               jac=_fc_jac, options=opt)
                if np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                    x_trial = np.clip(res.x, -8.0, 8.0).astype(np.float64)
                else:
                    x_trial = x_cur.copy()
            except Exception:
                x_trial = x_cur.copy()

            a_trial = softmax(x_trial @ basis)
            b_trial = fast_convolve(a_trial)
            s_trial = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(b_trial))
            if np.isfinite(s_trial) and s_trial < best_s:
                best_s = s_trial
                best_a = a_trial.copy()
                best_x = x_trial.copy()

            if np.isfinite(s_trial) and s_trial <= s_cur * 1.005:
                x_cur = x_trial
            else:
                if best_x is not None:
                    x_cur = best_x.copy()

    if best_a is None:
        if parent_a is not None:
            a = normalize_nonneg(parent_a)
            return a, float(score_norm(a))
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        return a, 2.0

    return best_a, float(best_s)


# ---- Exact projected gradient with incremental FFT convolution ----

def make_weights(b, fr):
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    L = len(b)
    if L == 0:
        return np.zeros(0)
    m = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L)
    temp = max(m * float(fr), 1e-300)
    z = (b - m) / temp
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z - zmax)
    ess = float(np.sum(e))
    if not np.isfinite(ess) or ess <= 0.0:
        e = np.zeros_like(b)
        e[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        ess = 1.0
    return e / ess


def exact_proj_grad(a0, time_limit, rng, verbose=False):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a0)
    n = len(a)
    b = fast_convolve(a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_s = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(b))

    if time_limit <= 0.0 or n <= 1:
        best_s = float(score_norm(best_a))
        return best_a, best_s

    t_end = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    lr = 0.7
    fr = 0.2
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    no_accept = 0

    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
        it += 1
        if it % 50 == 0:
            b = fast_convolve(a)
            b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)

        m = float(np.max(b))
        cur_s = 2.0 * n * m
        if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
            break

        if np.isfinite(cur_s) and cur_s < best_s - 1e-14:
            best_s = cur_s
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            no_accept = 0
            if verbose and it % 200 == 0:
                print(f"      exact-proj n={n} it={it} best={best_s:.6f}")
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        w = make_weights(b, fr)
        grad = 2.0 * cross_corr_weights(w, a)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-14:
            no_accept += 1
            if no_accept > 20:
                break
            continue

        g = grad / gnorm
        step = lr * float(np.linalg.norm(a))
        v = a - step * g
        p = simplex_project(v)
        d = p - a
        if float(np.max(np.abs(d))) < 1e-15:
            lr = max(1e-6, lr * 0.5)
            no_accept += 1
            if lr < 1e-8:
                break
            continue

        cd = fast_convolve(a, d)
        dd = fast_convolve(d, d)

        ts = [1.0, 0.9, 0.75, 0.6, 0.45, 0.3, 0.18, 0.1]
        best_t = 0.0
        best_sc = cur_s
        best_bt = None
        for tv in ts:
            bt = b + 2.0 * tv * cd + (tv * tv) * dd
            sc = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(bt))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
                best_sc = sc
                best_t = tv
                best_bt = bt

        if best_t > 0.0:
            a = a + best_t * d
            if np.min(a) < -1e-10:
                a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
                ss = float(a.sum())
                if ss <= 1e-15:
                    break
                a = a / ss
                b = fast_convolve(a)
                b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
            else:
                ss = float(a.sum())
                if abs(ss - 1.0) > 1e-10:
                    a = a / ss
                    b = fast_convolve(a)
                    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
                else:
                    b = best_bt
                    if np.min(b) < -1e-8:
                        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)

            if best_t >= 0.5:
                lr = min(4.0, lr * 1.08)
            else:
                lr = max(1e-6, lr * 0.75)
            no_accept = 0

            if np.isfinite(best_sc) and best_sc < best_s - 1e-14:
                best_s = best_sc
                best_a = a.copy()
        else:
            no_accept += 1
            lr = max(1e-6, lr * 0.6)
            if no_accept > 35:
                sigma = 0.08 * float(np.linalg.norm(a))
                a_p = normalize_nonneg(a + sigma * rng.standard_normal(n))
                b_p = fast_convolve(a_p)
                s_p = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(b_p))
                if np.isfinite(s_p) and s_p <= max(best_s * 1.05, s_p):
                    a = a_p
                    b = b_p
                else:
                    a = best_a.copy()
                    b = fast_convolve(a)
                    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
                no_accept = 0
                no_improve = 10
                lr = 0.7
                fr = min(2.0, fr * 1.6)

        if no_improve > 40:
            if no_improve % 40 == 0:
                fr = min(2.0, fr * 1.7)
                lr = max(0.2, lr * 0.8)
        elif no_improve < 10:
            fr = max(0.05, fr * 0.97)

        if no_improve > 320:
            if rng.random() < 0.6:
                a = best_a.copy()
                b = fast_convolve(a)
                b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
                no_improve = 0
                fr = 0.6
                lr = 0.7
            else:
                break

    b = fast_convolve(best_a)
    best_s = 2.0 * len(best_a) * float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(best_s):
        best_s = float(score_norm(best_a))
    return best_a, best_s


# ---- Initializers / helpers ----

def get_parent():
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if p.ndim != 1 or p.size == 0:
        return None
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
        return None
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return None
    return p / s


def interpolate_seq(f, new_n):
    f = normalize_nonneg(f)
    old_n = len(f)
    if old_n == new_n:
        return f.copy()
    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    out = np.interp(new_x, old_x, f)
    return normalize_nonneg(out)


def get_base_for_length(parent, L):
    if parent is not None and len(parent) > 0:
        return interpolate_seq(parent, L)
    return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)


def smooth_parent(a, rng, K=64, noise=0.02):
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    theta = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
    theta -= float(np.mean(theta))
    n = len(theta)
    F = np.fft.fft(theta)
    mmax = min(int(K), n // 2)
    mask = np.zeros(n, dtype=bool)
    if mmax > 0:
        mask[1:mmax + 1] = True
        start = n - mmax
        mask[start:n] = True
    F_low = F * mask
    theta_low = np.fft.ifft(F_low).real
    if noise > 0.0:
        theta_low = theta_low + float(noise) * rng.standard_normal(n)
    theta_low -= float(np.mean(theta_low))
    return softmax(theta_low)


def random_fourier_init(n, rng, Kmax=12, amp=1.0, noise=0.0):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    K = int(rng.integers(3, int(Kmax) + 1))
    theta = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, K + 1):
        am = float(amp) * float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.0)) / np.sqrt(float(m))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        theta += am * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * i / float(n) + ph)
    if noise > 0.0:
        theta += float(noise) * rng.standard_normal(n)
    theta -= float(np.mean(theta))
    return softmax(theta)


def phase_preset_init(n, rng):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = 0.9 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * i / float(n) + (np.pi / 4.0))
    theta += 0.4 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * i / float(n) + (3.0 * np.pi / 4.0))
    theta += 0.2 * np.cos(6.0 * np.pi * i / float(n) + float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)))
    theta += 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    theta -= float(np.mean(theta))
    return softmax(theta)


def update_best_seq(best_seq, best_score, a, label=""):
    if a is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    a = normalize_nonneg(a)
    s = float(score_norm(a))
    if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_score:
        if label:
            print(f"    NEW BEST [{label}] n={len(a)} score={s:.6f}")
        return a, s
    return best_seq, best_score


def propose_candidate():
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + 1780.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(20250725)

    best_seq = None
    best_score = np.inf

    parent = get_parent()
    if parent is not None:
        parent = normalize_nonneg(parent)
        s0 = float(score_norm(parent))
        if np.isfinite(s0):
            best_seq = parent.copy()
            best_score = s0
            print(f"Parent: n={len(parent)}, score={s0:.6f}")

    if best_seq is None:
        a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        best_seq = a
        best_score = 2.0
        print("No parent; using uniform 1024 as baseline.")

    lengths = []
    if parent is not None:
        lp = len(parent)
        if 256 <= lp <= 4096:
            lengths.append(lp)
    for L in [2048, 1024, 3072]:
        if L not in lengths:
            lengths.append(L)

    ordered = []
    for L in [2048, 1024, 3072]:
        if L in lengths and L not in ordered:
            ordered.append(L)
    for L in lengths:
        if L not in ordered:
            ordered.append(L)

    # -- Fourier-family seeds, especially at 2048/1024 --
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 1100.0:
        a_f, s_f = fourier_family_refine(2048, 8, 4, [0.4, 0.15], 90, rng,
                                         get_base_for_length(parent, 2048))
        best_seq, best_score = update_best_seq(best_seq, best_score, a_f, "fourier2048")

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 900.0:
        a_f, s_f = fourier_family_refine(1024, 7, 3, [0.4, 0.15], 80, rng,
                                         get_base_for_length(parent, 1024))
        best_seq, best_score = update_best_seq(best_seq, best_score, a_f, "fourier1024")

    # -- Build a modest pool of seeds for full L-BFGS --
    seeds = []
    for L in ordered[:3]:
        base = get_base_for_length(parent, L)
        seeds.append(base)
        if parent is not None:
            seeds.append(smooth_parent(base, rng, K=64, noise=0.02))
        seeds.append(random_fourier_init(L, rng, Kmax=12, amp=1.0, noise=0.02))
        seeds.append(phase_preset_init(L, rng))

    # -- Full-sequence L-BFGS refinement --
    for idx, seed in enumerate(seeds[:12]):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 650.0:
            break
        n_seed = len(seed)
        mi = 70 if n_seed <= 1200 else (55 if n_seed <= 2048 else 40)
        a_l, s_l = lbfgs_refine(
            seed,
            [0.5, 0.25, 0.1],
            mi,
            label=f"seed{idx}_n{n_seed}"
        )
        best_seq, best_score = update_best_seq(best_seq, best_score, a_l, f"lbfgs_seed{idx}")

    # -- Main exact projected-gradient polishing --
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    t_exact = min(750.0, max(120.0, rem * 0.55))
    a_e, s_e = exact_proj_grad(best_seq, t_exact, rng, verbose=True)
    best_seq, best_score = update_best_seq(best_seq, best_score, a_e, "exact_main")

    # -- Try neighboring lengths if still useful --
    if best_score > 1.5027:
        for L_try in [2048, 3072, 4096]:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 250.0:
                break
            if len(best_seq) == L_try:
                continue
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            t_try = min(160.0 if L_try <= 3072 else 110.0, max(30.0, rem * 0.45))
            a_try = interpolate_seq(best_seq, L_try)
            a_e2, s_e2 = exact_proj_grad(a_try, t_try, rng, verbose=False)
            best_seq, best_score = update_best_seq(best_seq, best_score, a_e2, f"exact_len{L_try}")
            if best_score <= 1.5027:
                break

    # -- Final short L-BFGS polish --
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 200.0:
        nn = len(best_seq)
        mi = 50 if nn <= 2048 else 32
        a_l2, s_l2 = lbfgs_refine(best_seq, [0.25, 0.1, 0.05], mi, label="final_lbfgs")
        best_seq, best_score = update_best_seq(best_seq, best_score, a_l2, "final_lbfgs")

    # -- Final exact polish --
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 40.0:
        t_final = max(15.0, min(220.0, rem - 20.0))
        a_final, s_final = exact_proj_grad(best_seq, t_final, rng, verbose=False)
        best_seq, best_score = update_best_seq(best_seq, best_score, a_final, "final_exact")

    # Fallback
    if best_seq is None or len(best_seq) == 0:
        best_seq = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        best_score = 2.0

    best_seq = normalize_nonneg(best_seq)
    try:
        final_score = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_seq]))
    except Exception:
        final_score = best_score

    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        best_seq = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        final_score = 2.0

    print(f"FINAL: n={len(best_seq)}, score={final_score:.6f}")
    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```
